- **otopcy-architecture**: Use when laying out a new codebase, deciding where server-only code lives, debugging a page that works in dev but 500s in production, setting up logging and request IDs, adding an optional third-party provider, validating environment variables, or deciding whether to add multi-tenancy. - **otopcy-audit**: Use before shipping to production, or when asked to audit, review or harden the security of an app — including a vibe-coded or AI-generated one, with or without access to the source. Runs 20 controls, ranks findings by real impact, and refuses to mark anything green without evidence. Also covers what to do when a key has been exposed (revoke first, investigate second). ([Giovannyengamba/Otopcy-Softstart](https://github.com/Giovannyengamba/Otopcy-Softstart/tree/HEAD/skills/otopcy-audit) · ★2)
- **otopcy-outils**: Use when choosing or connecting a third-party service — hosting, database, Redis, email, file storage, error tracking, analytics, real-time, video conferencing, payments — or when setting up Google properties (Cloud Console OAuth credentials, Search Console, Merchant Center, Tag Manager, Analytics, Ads), DNS and email authentication. - **otopcy-performance**: Use when a site or page is slow, when nothing is being cached, when adding ISR or cache tags, when a stale value keeps being served, when choosing a deployment region, or when writing background/scheduled work. - **otopcy-softstart**: Use when starting a new web project, or when making a structural decision on an existing one — folder layout, where server code lives, which provider to add, what to check before shipping. - **otros-agentes**: Delegar en Codex, Kimi o Cursor una tarea acotada (tests, refactor mecánico, docs, QA en navegador) o pedirles una segunda opinión, sin interacción y en un worktree propio, y revisar su resultado. Úsala para ahorrar cuota de Claude. ([thealejandro/claude-reglas](https://github.com/thealejandro/claude-reglas/tree/HEAD/skills/otros-agentes))
- **otros-imagen**: "Otros de imagen" agrupa cuatro herramientas sin parentesco técnico que cubren huecos que FLUX, Seedream y Qwen-Image no cubren igual: generación barata y rapidísima (Z-Image Turbo, Krea 2 Turbo), un todo-en-uno t2i+edición+personalización (HiDream O1 1.5) y un realce de resolución puro (Tencent Image Upscaler). Elige por tarea, no por "parecido" entre modelos: cada uno espera un estilo de prompt ([egodivergente/nexus](https://github.com/egodivergente/nexus/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/skills/models/otros-imagen) · ★1 · 2 copies)

- **ots**: Use this skill when the user wants to securely share secrets, create one-time URLs for sensitive data, or encrypt and share passwords securely. ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/ots/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **ots-init**: Scaffold okf/temporal under SECOND_BRAIN_ROOT. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series/tree/HEAD/skills/ots-init))
- **ots-inventory-auditor**: Monitors dependency manifests, cross-checks against docs/OTS_SOFTWARE.md, and scaffolds risk assessments. ([esanacore/engineering-constitution](https://github.com/esanacore/engineering-constitution/tree/HEAD/skills/ots-inventory-auditor) · ★2)
- **ots-prune**: Drop telemetry files older than 90 days. Pruning is a git commit of the working tree. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series/tree/HEAD/skills/ots-prune))
- **ots-session**: Open/close an isolated write session. Uses second-brain-core brain_session.py; does not fork the protocol. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series/tree/HEAD/skills/ots-session))
- **ots-summarize**: After tick-hour, read .source.jsonl, apply the host-switch filter, write summary and saliency. Edition A (host CLI) or B (API key). Does not touch the snapshot. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series/tree/HEAD/skills/ots-summarize))
- **ots-tail**: Copy a host JSONL transcript into an immutable sessions/&lt;slug&gt;.source.jsonl snapshot. No LLM. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series/tree/HEAD/skills/ots-tail))
- **ots-tick**: Scheduled hourly tick. Writes an Hour node only when the window has closed segments and no open ones. Sparse. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series/tree/HEAD/skills/ots-tick))
- **ots-validate**: Validate temporal nodes: type, period, aggregates, session identity. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series/tree/HEAD/skills/ots-validate))
- **ots-walk**: Walk Year → Month → Week → Day → Hour → Session using only the filesystem. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series/tree/HEAD/skills/ots-walk))
- **ots-watchdog**: Close sessions open longer than the global watchdog (default one hour). Per-role is phase two. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series/tree/HEAD/skills/ots-watchdog))
- **ots-write**: Commit a temporal node. The model proposes body text; this script writes frontmatter. ([SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/okf-time-series/tree/HEAD/skills/ots-write))
- **otsafescan-SKILL.md**: &gt; Perform vulnerability scanning in OT/ICS environments safely using passive monitoring, native protocol queries, and carefully controlled active scanning with Tenable OT Security to identify vulnerabilities without disrupting industrial processes or crashing legacy controllers. ([DCx7C5/ai-marketplace](https://github.com/DCx7C5/ai-marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/vuln/scanning/otsafescan) · 2 copies)
- **otsclear**: Capture any unpersisted decisions to disk, show cost summary, then prompt the user to /clear ([jpurusho/ots](https://github.com/jpurusho/ots/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/otsclear))
- **otsenka-podskazki-krupnee**: «Правильно ли сработал krupnee_lift», «разбери trace, диалог, feedback rows, GitHub Issue», «eval cases, telemetry krupnee», «lift предложен рано». Offline, не runtime. ([kir-kopylov/codex-team-skills](https://github.com/kir-kopylov/codex-team-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/team-skills/skills/otsenka-podskazki-krupnee) · ★1)
- **otsev-lozhnogo-uspeha-operatsii**: «Ложный success», «успех раньше времени», «честно ли запуск завершён», «все пути к завершению», «completion gate после инцидента». Covers core OTTR concepts plus the complete stOTTR grammar. Pair with the maplib skill when the templates are expanded over DataFrames in Python. - **otu-abundance-table-construction**: Use when quality-filtered amplicon reads (e.g., temp/filtered.fa) need to be mapped to a reference OTU database at 97% identity to produce a valid tab-delimited OTU count table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-abundance-table-construction))
- **otu-abundance-table-normalization**: Use when an OTU abundance table (e.g. .tsv, .csv or BIOM, such as HMP_SRA_gut_tiny.tsv) must be normalized automatically before FlashWeave-style statistical co-occurrence network inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/otu-abundance-table-normalization))
- **otu-annotation-consistency-analysis**: Use when OTUs were built by clustering 16S sequences (e.g. HMP V3V5/V1V3 FASTAs at 97% similarity) with a single representative sequence, and you need to measure how representative-sequence annotation propagates (or fails) across OTU members. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-annotation-consistency-analysis))
- **otu-asv-table-import**: Use when the input is an amplicon (16S rDNA) OTU/ASV abundance table from QIIME 2 or BIOM/TSV format and the analysis goal requires importing it into a functional-prediction or amplicon analysis pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-asv-table-import))
- **otu-clustering-97-percent-similarity**: Use when input is a length/quality-filtered amplicon FASTA (e.g. temp/filtered.fa) and the goal is to collapse redundant reads into representative OTU_ or ASV_ sequences before building an OTU table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-clustering-97-percent-similarity))
- **otu-clustering-against-annotated-database**: Use when you have chimera-filtered paired-end 16S reads (never merged) plus an annotated 16S FASTA database and need annotated OTUs at species-level resolution without merging reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/otu-clustering-against-annotated-database))
- **otu-copy-number-normalization**: Use when a 16S rRNA OTU table must be converted to per-organism abundances (correcting 16S copy-number variation) before predicting gene family (KO) abundances with PICRUSt. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/otu-copy-number-normalization))
- **otu-count-partition-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when tuning a de novo clustering partition count for long-read 16S amplicon data and you need to quantify the OTU-inflation vs. consensus-accuracy trade-off across partition settings (1–7) on mock or known-composition communities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/otu-count-partition-sensitivity-analysis))
- **otu-hierarchical-mapping**: Use when demultiplexed, quality-filtered rRNA amplicon or shotgun reads (FASTA) must be classified against a reference taxonomy and hierarchical OTU clusters simultaneously, with confidence values per level, to build OTU count tables or compare OTU consistency across sequencing runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-hierarchical-mapping))
- **otu-pipeline-performance-comparison**: Use when comparing how different quality-filtering strategies (MICCA's expected-error-based filtering vs UPARSE's truncation-based filtering) change retained-read and retained-chimera fractions on simulated 16S or ITS amplicon datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/otu-pipeline-performance-comparison))
- **otu-reference-construction-with-uclust**: Use when you need to test how the choice of reference OTU database (e.g. UCLUST-derived ab initio clusters vs. the default hOTU reference) affects MAPseq OTU mapping consistency between runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-reference-construction-with-uclust))
- **otu-relative-abundance-computation**: Use when comparing OTU-picking pipelines on data with a known composition (e.g. the 16S-R synthetic dataset of 500 OTUs and 100000 reads), and you need a quantitative abundance-accuracy metric rather than OTU-count or rarefaction curves. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/otu-relative-abundance-computation))
- **otu-sequence-fragment-insertion**: Use when OTU member sequences are short amplicon fragments (e.g., HMP V1V3 or V3V5 reads) that must be aligned to a fixed reference alignment before computing MSD between representative and member sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-sequence-fragment-insertion))
- **otu-site-occupancy-filtering**: Use when raw OTU count tables from eDNA metabarcoding (with PCR replicate detection data per sample) must be decontaminated before taxonomic grouping and downstream community analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-site-occupancy-filtering))
- **otu-species-reference-assignment**: Reference for validating OTU tables against ground-truth species inventories when OTUs must be labeled taxonomically (GenBank ITS2) and compared per-site to field survey lists to quantify recaptured, unregistered, redundant, and lost species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-species-reference-assignment))
- **otu-table-abundance-processing**: Use when an OTU/amplicon abundance table with unaltered Greengenes taxonomic assignments (read counts, not percentages) must be corrected for 16S rRNA gene copy number bias, or when 16S qPCR totals need GCN correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/otu-table-abundance-processing))
- **otu-table-curation**: Use when an OTU/ASV table from any clustering method (VSEARCH, SWARM, CROP, DADA2) shows inflated richness or taxonomic redundancy (e.g., many OTUs mapping to single-species genera like Fagus) and needs curation before α-diversity, redundancy, or beta-diversity analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-table-curation))
- **otu-table-qc-and-formatting**: Use when raw 16S amplicon data (e.g., mockrobiota mock4, mock12, mock16) must be converted into per-method feature tables that are QC-checked, formatted for qiime2, and compared to true community compositions via weighted UniFrac. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/otu-table-qc-and-formatting))
- **otu-table-salinity-gradient-trend-analysis**: Use when you have OTU tables for environmental samples ordered along a salinity gradient (e.g., river, lagoon, open sea 16S rRNA data) and need to test habitat differentiation via PERMANOVA and check the decreasing-OTU-with-salinity trend. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-table-salinity-gradient-trend-analysis))
- **otu-table-singleton-filtering**: Use when you have an initial OTU table (e.g., from VSEARCH, SWARM, CROP or DADA2) and want to remove single-read OTUs as a baseline noise-removal step, or to benchmark it against co-occurrence-based post-clustering curation such as LULU. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-table-singleton-filtering))
- **otu-taxonomic-classification-mapping**: Use when 16S amplicon reads (e.g. V3-V4 FASTQ from MiSeq, already adapter- and quality-trimmed) must be clustered into OTUs and classified taxonomically against SILVA SSU ref release 132 to build an abundance table for downstream diversity or differential-abundance tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/otu-taxonomic-classification-mapping))
